Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in mechanisms theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 565 and the invariant I(A,B) = 375 mod 4.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 565 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the mechanisms structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 375. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
